{"forms":[{"form":"more literally","tags":["comparative"]},{"form":"most literally","tags":["superlative"]},{"form":"lit.","tags":["abbreviation"]},{"form":"litterally","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adv","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"He's prone to exaggeration, so don't take what he says literally.","type":"example"},{"text":"There are literally millions of individual pieces of space debris orbiting Earth.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a literal manner; exactly as stated, word for word.","Without overstatement or understatement, or false or misleading words."],"synonyms":[{"word":"ad verbum"}]},{"antonyms":[{"word":"figuratively"},{"word":"idiomatically"},{"word":"metaphorically"},{"word":"virtually"}],"examples":[{"text":"When I saw on the news that there would be no school tomorrow because of the snowstorm, I literally jumped for joy, and hit my head on the ceiling fan.","type":"example"},{"text":"I didn't have time to repair the wall, so I literally just papered over the cracks.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a literal manner; exactly as stated, word for word.","With phrasings that might normally be used or understood as figurative: truly; not figuratively; not as an idiom or metaphor."],"synonyms":[{"word":"ad verbum"},{"word":"actually"},{"word":"really"},{"word":"unfiguratively"},{"word":"unmetaphorically"},{"word":"above a bit"},{"word":"actually"},{"tags":["idiomatic"],"word":"all things considered"},{"word":"as a matter of fact"},{"tags":["obsolete"],"word":"by right"},{"word":"certainly"},{"tags":["idiomatic"],"word":"come to think of it"},{"word":"de facto"},{"word":"definitely"},{"word":"de jure"},{"tags":["idiomatic"],"word":"fact is"},{"tags":["archaic","poetic"],"word":"forsooth"},{"word":"frankly"},{"word":"honestly"},{"word":"in actuality"},{"word":"in all honesty"},{"word":"indubitably"},{"word":"in fact"},{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"in faith"},{"word":"in point of fact"},{"word":"in reality"},{"word":"in truth"},{"word":"indeed"},{"word":"legitimately"},{"word":"literally"},{"word":"not gonna lie"},{"word":"properly"},{"word":"really"},{"word":"right"},{"word":"simply"},{"tags":["obsolete"],"word":"soothfast"},{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"soothly"},{"word":"surely"},{"word":"to be frank"},{"word":"to be honest"},{"word":"TBH"},{"word":"tbh"},{"tags":["idiomatic"],"word":"to tell the truth"},{"word":"truly"},{"word":"truthfully"},{"word":"undoubtedly"},{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"verily"},{"word":"very much"},{"tags":["dialectal"],"word":"wis"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"My daughter's pet rabbit had babies, and now we've literally got rabbits coming out of our ears.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In a literal manner; exactly as stated, word for word.","Draws attention to a pun or other wordplay involving an idiom."],"synonyms":[{"word":"ad verbum"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"I had no idea, so I was literally guessing.","type":"example"},{"text":"I was literally having breakfast when she arrived.","type":"example"},{"text":"She was literally like, \"What?\", and I was literally like, \"Yeah\".","type":"example"},{"text":"Literally who is this?","type":"example"}],"glosses":["As an intensifier.","Used as a general intensifier or dramatiser, sometimes tending towards a meaningless filler."],"tags":["colloquial"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He was so surprised, he literally jumped twenty feet in the air.","type":"example"},{"text":"On 9/11 people were literally glued to their TV sets.","type":"example"},{"text":"People can't even express their opinions freely in this country any more. Literally 1984!","type":"example"}],"glosses":["As an intensifier.","Used as an intensifier with statements or terms that are in fact meant figuratively and not word for word as stated."],"tags":["proscribed","sometimes"]},{"examples":[{"text":"It's not even hard⁠ to make—you literally just put it in the microwave for five minutes and it's done.","type":"example"},{"text":"It won't take me long to get back, 'cause the store's literally two blocks away.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Used as a generic downtoner: just, merely."],"synonyms":[{"word":"merely"},{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"barely"},{"word":"but"},{"word":"just"},{"word":"literally"},{"word":"merely"},{"word":"no more than"},{"word":"nothing but"},{"word":"only"},{"word":"simply"}],"tags":["colloquial"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈlɪ.tə.ɹə.li/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɪ.tɹə.li/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɪ.tə.ɹə.li/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɪ.tɹə.li/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈlɪ.təɹ.li/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":251435,"upstream_offset":1247473022,"upstream_record_sha256":"87344a4cfaf2387e2d1b4d95b3234e372f66cc392934e83b78ab4ce4afdd0e73","word":"literally"}
